Its meaning — *"an arm RELATIVE TO its same-seed mates"* — implies a testable **NEGATIVE**: the column must actually VARY inside a (gene, seed_family) cell. Implemented, and it **immediately flagged SEVEN columns constant within every one of the 295 multi-arm cells**: `arm_dbeta` · `arm_sep_z` · `arm_resolvable` · `n_arm_in_cell` · `oof_rho_arm` · `oof_rho_fam` · `oof_drho`. They describe the **CELL**, not the arm within it ⇒ relabelled **`family`** rung. ⚠⚠ **I had hand-identified only THREE of the seven** — `n_arm_in_cell` and the three `oof_*` would have been missed. **Build the check; do not hand-move the columns.** The four that genuinely vary inside a cell (`arm_credit_share` 467/467, `arm_id_status` 22/467, `family_dose_share`, `family_role`) keep `arm-in-family`. **(2) ⭐ THE HLY-LEG DISAGREEMENT IS NOW GATEABLE.** `arm_lfc_HLY_TUM_QN` and `_raw` are nominally the same comparison but correlate at only **+0.544**, median |diff| **1.49**, and **disagree on SIGN for 23.9%** of the 3,433 edges carrying both — so a reader's arbitrary choice of leg flips the direction for ~1 edge in 4, silently, since both columns look equally authoritative. New `hly_leg_concordant` (edge rung, derived, no refit): **concordant 76.1% / discordant 23.9%**. ⛔ **Explicitly NOT a correctness flag** — agreement does not make either leg right; it is a DISAGREEMENT flag, which is what the data supports. ⚠ Both HLY legs cross **GTEx→TCGA**; only `arm_lfc_NAT_TUM` is same-platform. **(3) Registered and documented** — `hly_leg_concordant` added to `_EDGE_ONLY` (edge rung: the two legs are compared row-wise, so it is NOT an arm property). **Final: 713 columns, `--check` CLEAN, `card_scan` 0/0, glossary 713/713, 0 duplicate keys, browser rebuilt.** |
| column review UNIT 6 — `edge` / `ctx_` (18 cols) + ⭐ THE BROADCAST COST QUANTIFIED | 2026-08-19 | **ok — all 18 labels correct; a ~6× hazard measured and promoted to doctrine** | ~25 min | Sixth review unit: the gene-design context block broadcast onto edge rows. **(1) ✅ ALL 18 RUNG LABELS CORRECT.** 16 are gene-rung and constant within gene; the 2 exceptions (`ctx_arm_dose`, `ctx_arm_abundant`) are declared **arm** rung and are constant within ARM across genes (**0/737** violations each). ⚠ **My first test flagged those two as mismatches because I applied the GENE invariance to an ARM-rung column** — the labels were fine and the TEST was wrong. Same class as MH-192 (a verifier that was never itself verified); caught by reading the declared rung before trusting my own check. **(2) ⭐⭐ THE REAL FINDING — THE COST OF A BROADCAST COLUMN, MEASURED RATHER THAN ASSERTED.** Everyone knows a coarse-rung column repeats down the rows; what was never quantified is what that does to a statistic computed OVER those rows. **A row-wise statistic over a broadcast column weights each parent unit by HOW MANY ROWS IT HAS** — on the edge card, by the gene's **DESIGN WIDTH** (5,649 rows / 1,420 genes, up to **91 edges on one gene**). And ceiling, dose, abundance and the decoy gap all TRACK width ⇒ **the weighting is not a harmless imprecision, it IS the confound.** Same column, two rungs, two answers: **`ctx_ceiling` 0.0809 (edge rows) vs 0.0138 (gene rows) — ~6×** · `ctx_n_abund` 4.00 vs 1.00 (4×) · `ctx_dose_max` 13.06 vs 8.97 · `ctx_gap_deconv` −0.0196 vs −0.0085 (2.3×) · `ctx_gap_core` −0.0186 vs −0.0148. ⭐ **PROMOTED TO `CARD_RUNG_DOCTRINE`** as a general rule with the table, because it applies to EVERY broadcast column, not just `ctx_`: *compute a broadcast column's statistic on the card that OWNS its rung, or de-duplicate to one row per parent first.* Two of these are load-bearing — the decoy gap and the measurability ceiling — and a 6× shift in the ceiling would silently move any claim conditioned on measurability. **(3) ✅ SELF-AUDIT PASSED.** This unit re-checked my own recent claims, since `ctx_ceiling` and `ctx_gap_core` carried the decoy stratification and the `n_identified` gradients. **The MH-248 stratification used the GENE card and reproduces EXACTLY (n=1,349, median −0.0148)**; on edge rows it would have read −0.0186. Right answer, but by having used the right card rather than by having checked — which is why the rule is now written down. **(4) DESCRIBED** the under-documented design-match columns: `ctx_collapse` (mean arms merged per family, median 1.07 — near-1 means the collapse was nearly a no-op), `ctx_d_fam` and `ctx_d_collin` (the two MH-167 design-match asymmetries, both bounded at ≤0.0025 ρ), and `ctx_gap_d_dose` (the ONE design axis with a detectable effect, already corrected in the headline). **VERDICTS: KEEP 18 · REDESCRIBE 8.** Final: **713 columns, `--check` CLEAN, glossary 713/713, 0 duplicate keys, browser rebuilt.** |
| column review UNIT 7 — `edge` / `beta_` (9 cols, 8 cross-card) | 2026-08-19 | **ok — two "fraction" columns pinned to E[ratio] vs ratio-of-E; the inert gate exonerated** | ~25 min | Seventh unit: the model's own posterior block, 8 of 9 columns cross-card (edge here, family on `gene_family`). **(1) ⭐⭐ `beta_frac` AND `beta_frac_abs` ARE NOT THE SAME SHARE, AND THE DIFFERENCE IS JENSEN — not a bug, and not documented anywhere until now.** β is strictly positive since MH-124, so β/Σβ and |β|/Σ|β| ought to coincide — yet they differ on **4,985 of 5,644 rows**, max **0.112**. Reading the producer settles it: **`beta_frac` = `nanmean` over Gibbs DRAWS of (β_f/Σβ) — E[ratio]**, computed inside the sampler with a companion `beta_frac_sd`; **`beta_frac_abs` = |E[β_f]| / Σ|E[β]| — the ratio of E**, computed in `add_reliability` from the already-summarised point estimates. ⭐ **MECHANISM CONFIRMED, not asserted: spearman(`beta_frac_sd`, |gap|) = +0.809 (n=5,644)**, and the median gap rises monotonically with posterior width — tight **0.00005** → mid 0.00039 → wide 0.00174 → very wide **0.00844**, a **169× spread**. ⇒ the gap IS the posterior width. **Use `beta_frac` + `beta_frac_sd` when uncertainty matters; `beta_frac_abs` when a plain bounded share of the point estimates is wanted.** ✅ Controls: `beta_frac_abs` reconstructs β/Σβ over the gene's ARMS to 5e-4 (rounding), and BOTH sum to 1 within gene on **100%** of the 1,420 genes. **(2) ✅ `beta_frac_reliable` IS EXONERATED — constant TRUE is the CORRECT answer here.** Unlike the `arb_max_identity` case, this gate is not failing at its job: MH-119 built it because `beta_frac` reached 999% when a gene's βs cancelled, MH-124 then found those negative βs were a **sampler bug** and fixed it, so β is now strictly positive (min +0.00096) and `beta_frac` is properly bounded **[0.0015, 1.0000] with 0 rows above 1**. **The defect it guards can no longer occur.** ⇒ vestigial, a prune candidate, and explicitly NOT evidence that a gate was applied to something needing one. Documented with the contrast to `identity_reliable`, which guards the cancellation that DOES still occur — in `identity`, not in β. **(3) VERIFIED THE REST:** `beta_sd_deconv` genuinely differs from `beta_sd` (5,597/5,644 rows, corr 0.999) so neither substitutes for the other; `retention_beta` ≈ `beta_deconv`/`beta` (max|diff| 0.0124, rounding) and exceeds 1 for **41.3%** of edges — the same adjustment-increases-the-effect pattern the family card shows, and the reason `retention_reliable` earns its keep there; `beta_arm` (fill 0.203) is the within-cell arm β and genuinely varies per arm in 467/467 cells, unlike its cell-constant neighbours. **VERDICTS: KEEP 9 · REDESCRIBE 7 · PRUNE-CANDIDATE 1 (`beta_frac_reliable`, left in place as a record that the check was made).** Final: **713 columns, `--check` CLEAN, glossary 713/713, 0 duplicate keys, browser rebuilt.** |

Both are constant, and `net_pressure` was additionally bit-identical to `pip_dense`. ⚠ **A constant guard is a SILENT guard** — deleting one would lose the alarm, so the condition is now **ASSERTED in `readouts.add_reliability`**: if β sign-cancellation ever returns it PRINTS, which a constant column never would. `pip_dense` is deliberately KEPT — also constant, but it records the π≡1 DESIGN CHOICE rather than a defunct check. ⚠ My retirement edit mangled three glossary keys into a syntax error; caught immediately by the import failing, repaired, and the duplicate-key scan re-run clean. **(2) COLUMN REVIEW UNIT 8 — the `cov_` coverage flags, which decide whether every OTHER block reads as `0` or `— not scanned`.** ✅ **THE BOUND MAPPINGS ARE EXACT: all 16 hold at precision 1.000 / recall 1.000**, against a design bar of ≥0.995 in BOTH directions (`flag False ⟺ block entirely blank`). **(3) ⭐⭐ AND THE NAMES LIE — which is the whole case for the map being MEASURED rather than curated, now demonstrated rather than argued.** A hand-written flag→block table would bind by name and be WRONG for most: `cov_famrole`→`famrole_` recall **0.911** · `cov_attr`→`attr_` **0.750** · `cov_ago_dom`→`ago_` **0.461** · `cov_comp`→`cload_` **0.139** · `cov_meth`→`bc_` **0.354** — every one fails the bar. ⭐ **`attr_` is in fact governed by `cov_beta` (1.000/1.000), NOT by `cov_attr`.** ⇒ the "guessed mapping fabricates a provenance claim" warning in `gen_cards` is not hypothetical; five same-named pairs would have been mis-bound. **(4) ⚠⚠ THE GUARANTEE IS PARTIAL, AND THAT WAS NOT RECORDED ANYWHERE.** Only **16 of the arm card's 42 blocks** have a governing flag, and **8 of the 21 flags govern nothing** (`cov_ago_dom`, `cov_attr`, `cov_comp`, `cov_cptac`, `cov_expr`, `cov_famrole`, `cov_meth`, `cov_subtype`). ⇒ **for the other 26 blocks — including `fame_` (28 cols), `adm_`, `chim_`, and the `fst_`/`disc_` blocks I added this session — a blank CANNOT be distinguished from a measured zero.** The three-empty-states promise covers 38% of the card, not the card. Re-run on **`coupling_fam` (99.9% complete, spearman +0.901 with `coupling_tum`)**: seedless **−0.0024** → site only **−0.0130** → site+1 chimeric **−0.0983** → site+2 **−0.1086**, **spearman(rung, coupling) = −0.1909** versus the originally reported **−0.2412**. ⇒ **the ladder STANDS and the direction is unchanged, but the effect is ~20% smaller on complete coverage — quote −0.19 as the coverage-robust figure.** ✅ **`coupling_fam` is now documented as the coverage-robust stand-in**: it supplies an answer for **1,570 edges that have none** at arm level. **VERDICTS: KEEP 13 · REDESCRIBE 5.** Final: **710 columns, `--check` CLEAN, glossary 710/710, 0 duplicate keys, browser rebuilt.** |
| ⛔⛔ `coupling_tum`'s 27.9% MISSINGNESS IS **STALENESS**, NOT A DATA PROPERTY — retracts my own unit-9 read | 2026-08-19 | **⚠ DEFECT FOUND — `edge_card_base.tsv` is 15 days stale; 4 hypotheses refuted before the right test** | ~30 min | User: *"what exactly was missing from the coupling? why? what about the seedless?"* — I had reported the CORRELATES and never the CAUSE, and chasing it refuted my own write-up. **(1) FOUR HYPOTHESES TESTED AND REFUTED:** ⛔ arm absent from the gene's design — **0%** of missing edges (all 52 sampled had the arm in `X`); ⛔ zero variance after residualisation — **0%** (arms carry sd≈0.92, ~1,028 distinct values); ⛔ whole-gene failure — only **9.8%** of missing edges are in wholly-missing genes, so **90% is per-edge inside partially-missing genes**; ⛔ NaN propagation through `spearmanr` — **0%** of either group has any NaN. **(2) ⭐ THE DECISIVE TEST WAS TO RECOMPUTE.** Of **25** edges whose card value is NaN, **24 produce a finite coupling now** — including **PTEN→miR-19b-3p = −0.3436**, a canonical strong edge simply absent from the card. ⇒ **the value was computable and was not stored.** **(3) THE CAUSE: `edge_card_base.tsv` is dated 2026-08-04** and `canonical_card.build()` has not been re-run since, while the design/expression inputs moved on. `realization/edge_card.tsv` therefore layers today's annotations over a **15-day-old base** — the delivered card is a MIX of fresh and stale columns. Same defect class as **MH-204's stale decoy table**, and the same tell: **a suspiciously structured missingness is a PROVENANCE question before it is a data question** (memory `replace-migrate-archive`). **(4) ⛔ RETRACTS MY UNIT-9 CLAIM.** I reported `coupling_tum` as *"missing not at random — concentrated on wide, well-measured genes (n_fam 10 vs 6; ceiling 0.124 vs 0.068) and seedless edges (55.7% vs 20%)"* and drew a measurability moral from it. **Those correlates describe WHAT CHANGED between 2026-08-04 and now, not a property of the measurement** — the seedless excess is most likely the arm-universe expansion since the base build. Glossary corrected with the retraction in place. **(5) WHAT SURVIVES, AND WHAT DOES NOT:** the evidence-ladder re-check stands as a COMPLETENESS comparison (`coupling_fam` reproduces the climb at spearman **−0.1909**) — ⚠ **but `coupling_fam` comes from the SAME stale base, so it controls for completeness and NOT for freshness. Both need re-deriving after a rebuild.** ⬜ **ACTION REQUIRED:** re-run `canonical_card.build()` → `realization.edge_card()` → `card_ladders --annotate` → `card_rungs --check` → `gen_cards --build`. **Until then treat every `coupling_*`, `share_*`, `rank_*`, `shift_class` and `arm_lfc_*` value as 2026-08-04 vintage, and do not read their missingness as biology.** ⚠ **NOT run here** — it is a substantial multi-worker rebuild that would move numbers several open analyses cite; the user should choose when. |

They correlate at spearman **0.828** where both exist but sit **1.78× apart in scale** and **3× apart in coverage** ⇒ a mixed statistic over them is meaningless.
